🎯 Goals & Motivation
  • Recomp: burn while protecting the lean. BF 40.1% is the 77th percentile for her age, so fat is the thing to move; ALMI at the 58th is respectable and slipping.

  • Why: flatter stomach, smaller waist, bigger legs and glutes, a feminine curvy look, and proving real fat loss to her dad. He sat in on Sep 2 and is the live audience for these numbers.
  • Taekwondo opens her up. Her dad, Sep 2: "she kicks off her heart. Power."
🏆 Wins to Open With Scan Pending
  • 8 for 8 between the Sep 2 scan and the cold. The 58% on the card is illness plus Disneyland (Sep 12), not adherence.
  • Loads climbed fast after your Sep 8 "add weight, not reps" note. Barbell bench 20×12 on Sep 7 to 64×8 on Sep 17. Full run in Lifting Metrics.
  • She fixed the barbell logging the same evening you asked. Sep 15 the front squat went in at 53 instead of 10, and it has stayed there.
  • 575 Macros Journal entries, and she logged on Sep 29 while sick. Complete days land 130 to 159 g protein, including 156 on Sep 23 and exactly 151 on Sep 24.
  • She solves her own problems. Sep 20, unprompted, she swapped pull-ups for lat pulldowns.

🎯 Scan-Day Focus
  1. Frame the window before any number. 27 days, Sep 7 to Oct 4, and say plainly that the read is fat versus lean, not the scale.
  2. Open on the two months, not the four weeks. Fat down 10.41 lb, body fat down 2.5 points, percentile 81st to 67th, trunk fat down 2.92 lb in the last block alone. That is a real transformation and it is hers.
  3. Then go straight at lean. If it fell again, own it as our call on intake: she trained six days a week and did everything asked in the gym, so a lean loss is a fuelling decision we got wrong, not effort she withheld. If it held, name it as the double scoop landing.
  4. Say the number out loud on protein. She cleared 170 on four days out of twenty-six and averaged about 119. She has proved she can do it, 181 on Sep 22. The gap is about 50 g a day and it is one more solid meal, not a diet change.
  5. More food, not less. Sub-1,200 days are the mechanism behind the lean loss, and she is on retatrutide where missed meals bring dizziness. No calorie number to her, but the direction is up.
  6. Lead with the honesty, not just the numbers. She told Daniel about the stomach illness and the double-job stretch the same day he asked, and four sessions were missed in nine days (Sep 25, 26, 28, 30). Credit the disclosure before getting into the shortfall.
  7. Handle visceral fat carefully. Compare to Jul 6, not Aug 6. The strategy is already sent and the box is ticked, so this is a read of direction, not a new protocol.
  8. Teach set one is the hardest. Six lifts sitting flat across all sets. Show her the lat pulldown on Sep 21, 70, 70, 60, as the shape she already got right once.
  9. Knee and the barbell squat. Two weeks of hack squat is up. Check whether it settled before putting the barbell back, and flag the Oct 21 assessment as where the pattern gets looked at.
  10. Open with her own Sep 27 words. The lower back fat coming down noticeably, and the scrub top that used to feel suffocating now loose. She noticed before she knew what the scan would say.
  11. Leave out: bone density (27 days is noise), any calorie target, the ALMI kg/m² value, and the bench, squat and deadlift Gold Standards.
  12. No renewal question, no close, no Slack post. Routine package checkpoint inside a term running to Jul 6, 2027.
